# Agents · A · part 41

944 entries, auditor-reliability to auto-task. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **auditor-reliability**: 信頼性監査担当。再現性（NFR-001）、テスト品質（NFR-020）、エラーハンドリング（P-010）の観点で独立監査する。コードは変更しない。静的解析エラー検証（最優先）→ Serena セマンティック検証 → 信頼性観点 の順で監査する。 ([KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template](https://github.com/KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-reliability.md))
- **auditor-rendiciones**: Audita un CSV de rendiciones extraído por /extractor-rendiciones. Entrega un resumen global (totales, conteos, duplicados, no procesados, diferencia vs hoja resumen) y hallazgos fila por fila en tres niveles - CRITICO, ADVERTENCIA, INFO. ([taxtic/claude-plugins-taxtic](https://github.com/taxtic/claude-plugins-taxtic/blob/HEAD/plugins/contabilidad-rendiciones/agents/auditor-rendiciones.md))
- **auditor-rendimiento**: Auditor de rendimiento de Fallen Legends 3D (móvil gl_compatibility, 40 criaturas animadas, web). Úsalo cuando se toque cualquier `_tick_*`, spawn, efectos, materiales, decoración o assets, cuando bajen los FPS o antes de exportar. Solo lee y mide; nunca modifica ficheros. ([JohanHdez/fallen-legends-3d](https://github.com/JohanHdez/fallen-legends-3d/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-rendimiento.md))
- **auditor-rendimiento-escena**: Audita los archivos de un proyecto de Unity o Godot (escenas, prefabs, ajustes de importación) para encontrar las causas reales del bajo rendimiento y de las builds pesadas. Úsalo cuando un juego o experiencia 3D vaya lento, caliente los móviles, tenga una build enorme o antes de enseñar una demo a un cliente o subirla a una tienda. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/juegos-alternativas-2/agents/auditor-rendimiento-escena.md))
- **auditor-retencion**: Úsalo cuando el usuario tenga vídeos publicados con analíticas disponibles (exportación CSV de retención, capturas del gráfico de retención o cifras manuales) y quiera diagnosticar dónde y por qué se va el público y qué cambiar en el próximo vídeo. Ejemplos: "por que se van en el segundo 8", "analiza la retencion de mis reels", "que hago distinto en el proximo video". ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/video-varios/agents/auditor-retencion.md))
- **auditor-rig-y-animacion**: Úsalo cuando un personaje con rig se comporte mal tras importarlo (mala deformación, pies que se deslizan, orientación incorrecta, retargeting roto desde Mixamo, huesos que faltan para Unity Humanoid), o antes de entregar o vender un personaje con rig y animado. Funciona con archivos FBX, GLB/GLTF y .blend. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/juegos-assets/agents/auditor-rig-y-animacion.md))
- **auditor-roxygen**: Audits every roxygen2 doc block in R/paintr-*.R for honest alignment with the actual source (params, defaults, returns, exports, examples, inherited params, behavioral prose). Report-only — never edits source. Use when the user wants a roxygen↔code truth audit. ([willju-wangqian/ggpaintr](https://github.com/willju-wangqian/ggpaintr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-roxygen.md) · ★1)
- **auditor-rutas**: Verificador mecánico de rutas, mounts y URLs en documentos y código. Dado un conjunto de archivos modificados, comprueba que cada ruta de filesystem mencionada existe en el árbol real, que cada URL pública declarada resuelve contra un mount existente en `server/server.js`, y que no se cuelan rutas legacy ya retiradas. Reporta hallazgos, NO corrige. ([adminmc2/prisma_consul](https://github.com/adminmc2/prisma_consul/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-rutas.md))
- **auditor-scout**: Breadth-first audit scout. Use to sweep a change (or the repo) for logic, security, and invariant risks across the whole touched surface, grounded in the explorer memory and the diff. Read-only; writes findings to disk. Use proactively during /auditor:run. ([hafizmirhamza276-lab/backend-agentic-marketplace](https://github.com/hafizmirhamza276-lab/backend-agentic-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/auditor/agents/auditor-scout.md))
- **auditor-secretos-dependencias**: Revisa un repositorio o una web propia buscando secretos filtrados, dependencias desactualizadas y cabeceras de seguridad ausentes, y devuelve un plan de arreglo priorizado. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/seguridad/agents/auditor-secretos-dependencias.md))
- **auditor-secretos-dependencias-en**: Reviews a repository or your own website looking for leaked secrets, outdated dependencies, and missing security headers, and returns a prioritized fix plan. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/en-agents-2/agents/auditor-secretos-dependencias-en.md))
- **auditor-security**: adversarial-review skill'inin üçüncü ve son geçişi. Attacker'ın bulgularını ve Defender'ın önerdiği düzeltmeleri bağımsız değerlendirir, severity atar, kabul/red kararı verir. Taraf tutmaz — ikisinin de çıktısını görür ama her ikisinden de bağımsız yargı üretir. Standart auditor rolünden (agent-orchestration) farklı olarak sadece güvenlik/istismar odaklı konfigürasyonlarda kullanılır. ([asistan-merkezi/claude-config](https://github.com/asistan-merkezi/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-security.md))
- **auditor-security**: セキュリティ監査担当。禁止操作（P-001）、秘密情報禁止（P-002）、依存関係（P-040）の観点で独立監査する。コードは変更しない。policy_check.py と重複してもよいので、目視で再確認する。 ([KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template](https://github.com/KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-security.md))
- **auditor-security**: セキュリティ監査担当。禁止操作（P-001）、秘密情報禁止（P-002）、依存関係（P-040）の観点で独立監査する。コードは変更しない。 ([KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template](https://github.com/KosGit-Dev/ai-dev-template/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/auditor-security.agent.md))
- **auditor-security**: Offensive security reviewer for anything handling untrusted input, credentials, or user data. Adopts an attacker's goals and gives the concrete attack path — trust boundaries and missing validation, injection in every form the stack permits, authn/authz gaps and privilege escalation, secret handling and exposure, supply-chain and dependency trust, data exfiltration. States what is out of scope because it could not be tested. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-security.md))
- **auditor-security**: The software kit's auditor seat. Judges the one risk that made a change lane 3, reading the change as an attacker would, and returns a verdict for the exact tree it read. Seated in lane 3 only. ([SaltyBrett/agentic-dev-template](https://github.com/SaltyBrett/agentic-dev-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-security.md) · 2 copies)
- **auditor-seguranca**: Auditor de segurança defensivo pré-entrega. Use proactively when the user asks to audit code, do a security review, find vulnerabilities, harden code before deploy, or apply security fixes. Encapsulates the IA Security Skill audit workflow (phases 0–7, including the secure/less-secure/insecure posture map) and returns a single Markdown report. Remediation (phase 8) is delegated to the main agent. Useful for chained workflows (audit → apply Critical fixes → re-audit) or to isolate the long report from the main conversation context. ([antoniocostalopes/ia-security-skill](https://github.com/antoniocostalopes/ia-security-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-seguranca.md) · ★6)
- **auditor-seguranca**: Analise PROFUNDA de seguranca, com SCANNER EXECUTADO. Acionar quando o diff abre superficie nova (entrada nao-confiavel, autenticacao/autorizacao, cripto/segredo, deserializacao, parse de arquivo, sink de DOM) OU mexe em dependencia (manifesto/lockfile). Roda ruff --select S, pip-audit, npm audit e busca de segredo no historico; depois faz taint e threat model sobre o que a ferramenta apontou. Padroes de vuln de CODIGO com cara de CRUD (IDOR, mass-assignment) sao do revisor-codigo, em todo diff. Read-only, nunca corrige. ([MrSchrodingers/tollens](https://github.com/MrSchrodingers/tollens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **auditor-seguranca**: Audita a segurança de um repositório seu por análise estática e devolve o laudo com achados priorizados + plano de ação. Use quando disser "audita a segurança do repo X", "procura falha de segurança", "confere os buracos antes de subir", ou quando a skill auditoria-seguranca precisar de varredura maior que 3 buscas. Só lê o seu próprio código — nunca ataca sistema no ar, nunca toca em ativo de terceiro, nunca altera código ou servidor. ([douglas21grcont-wq/Douglasg.castro_Skills](https://github.com/douglas21grcont-wq/Douglasg.castro_Skills/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Auditor de segurança (deixa o site seguro). Revisa back-end, front-end e configuração contra o checklist OWASP do projeto, corrige achados de gravidade Alta/Média e escreve docs/AUDITORIA-SEGURANCA.md. Usar antes de cada deploy. ([frankalcantara/Avalia2026-2](https://github.com/frankalcantara/Avalia2026-2/blob/HEAD/P26-3A-5E56-230D-1716-D540/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Use ANTES de fazer commit em qualquer arquivo que toque financeiro/, auth/, tenant/, kms/, migrations/, .claude/hooks/ ou .github/workflows/. Bloqueia commit se SEC-*, INV-TENANT-* ou SEC-TENANT-* de REGRAS-INEGOCIAVEIS.md forem violados. Camada A do híbrido — camada B é o GitHub Action equivalente em PR. ([roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao](https://github.com/roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Auditor especializado em segurança. Verifica LGPD (LGPD-001..010), secrets (SEC-001), vulnerabilidades OWASP Top 10, supply chain, permissões, criptografia. Use no /auditoria ou antes de subir mudança que toca em autenticação, dados pessoais, ou superfície externa. ([roldaobatista/roldao-method](https://github.com/roldaobatista/roldao-method/blob/HEAD/templates/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Auditoria read-only de segredos, PII, padrões inseguros e dependências. Usar proativamente em toda validação semanal e antes de tornar qualquer conteúdo público. ([tihh07/tihh07](https://github.com/tihh07/tihh07/blob/HEAD/plugins/fundacao/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Audita segurança do visual-similarity-engine (JWT, SSRF no download de thumbnails, SQL, vazamento de erro, dependências). Use para "audite a segurança", "tem vulnerabilidade aqui?". ([vximoraes/visual-similarity-engine](https://github.com/vximoraes/visual-similarity-engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca**: Use PROATIVAMENTE para revisar segurança de código novo ou existente — antes de publicar, ao tocar em autenticação/sessão/upload/API, ou quando o usuário pedir auditoria. Faz revisão adversarial (red team) cobrindo injeção, XSS, CSRF, SSRF, authz, vazamento e supply chain. ([wmgoncalves/Assistente_Lumina](https://github.com/wmgoncalves/Assistente_Lumina/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguranca.md))
- **auditor-seguranca-lgpd**: Auditor de segurança e conformidade LGPD com foco no tratamento interno de dados. - **auditor-seguridad**: Audita un cambio de CIMENTA desde la seguridad — autorización por obra contra IDOR, inyección, secretos, sesión y CSRF, URL prefirmadas y tipo leído de los bytes, dependencias con CVE. - **auditor-seguridad**: Revisión de seguridad independiente. Solo lectura. ([multiserviagua-pixel/Nido](https://github.com/multiserviagua-pixel/Nido/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguridad.md))
- **auditor-seguridad**: Solo dentro de una orquestación que el usuario inició con /orquesta:arquitecto (existe .orquesta/PLAN.md); fuera de ella no lo elijas por tu cuenta. Auditoría de seguridad de solo lectura para tareas del protocolo orquesta que tocan acceso a datos, SQL (SQL Server u Oracle), entrada de usuario, APIs, autenticación o secretos. Busca inyección (SQL, comando, LDAP, log, path traversal, SSRF), parametrización, menor privilegio, manejo de errores que filtre y secretos fuera de Key Vault. Dictamina APROBADO o RECHAZADO con archivo:línea. Complementa al revisor; no lo reemplaza. ([royrojas/Claude-Orquesta](https://github.com/royrojas/Claude-Orquesta/blob/HEAD/plugins/orquesta/agents/auditor-seguridad.md))

Solo lee; nunca modifica ficheros. ([JohanHdez/fallen-legends-3d](https://github.com/JohanHdez/fallen-legends-3d/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seguridad-licencias.md))
- **auditor-senior**: Agente Auditor Senior de Arquitetura e Qualidade (Staff/Principal). Analisa SOLID item a item, YAGNI, DRY, SSOT, non-inventing, code smells e riscos de manutencao. - **auditor-seo**: Audita el SEO de PremadeGG en el código Angular y en la web publicada (indexación de la SPA, títulos y meta por ruta, Open Graph, sitemap, robots, datos estructurados, Core Web Vitals). Solo lectura; usar cuando se quiera mejorar el posicionamiento o antes de publicar páginas nuevas. ([MattiAR0/premade-gg](https://github.com/MattiAR0/premade-gg/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-seo.md) · ★1)
- **auditor-seo**: Audita el SEO técnico y la visibilidad en IA (GEO) del portafolio — title y description por idioma, canonical y hreflang es/en, Open Graph, JSON-LD (Person, ProfilePage, proyectos), sitemap, robots.txt, llms.txt y las versiones Markdown. Usalo cuando se pida mejorar el posicionamiento, revisar meta tags o datos estructurados, o antes de publicar una página o un proyecto nuevo. Ejemplos — "mejorá el SEO", "revisá los meta tags", "¿por qué no aparezco en Google?", "¿ChatGPT entiende mi portafolio?". - **auditor-servidor**: Audita el endurecimiento de un servidor Linux de CIMENTA y hace pruebas de penetración contra el OWASP Top 10 y el ASVS — SSH, cortafuegos, actualizaciones, usuarios, permisos, servicios expuestos, TLS, registros, límites de recursos, respaldos y contenedores. Solo lee y reporta hallazgos con su severidad; no modifica el servidor y solo actúa sobre los servidores propios que nombra la guía de operación. Úsalo cuando se pida revisar la seguridad o la disponibilidad de un servidor, antes de exponer un entorno y después de un despliegue, siempre desde `/sdd-revisar-servidor`. ([FranciscoRodSilva/IA4Devs-Proyecto-Final](https://github.com/FranciscoRodSilva/IA4Devs-Proyecto-Final/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/auditor-servidor.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **auditor-simplicity**: The complexity prosecutor. Finds complexity that buys nothing — reinvented stdlib/platform features, single-implementation abstractions, speculative generality (YAGNI), dependencies added for what a few lines would do, indirection a reader must decode, and dead code. Names the simpler construct that replaces each and the lines it deletes. Deletion is the fix. Ignores correctness and security. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-simplicity.md))
- **auditor-simplify**: Use proactively when the cycle needs simplify / dead code audits — duplicated helpers, dead exports, unnecessary abstractions, silent fallback violations, rule drift. Returns 1-3 high-confidence findings under 400 words. ([g-kari/rss](https://github.com/g-kari/rss/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-simplify.md) · ★1)
- **auditor-sincronia-2d**: Auditor de sincronía entre Fallen Legends 3D y el juego 2D (arena-arpg): copias literales (map_builder.gd, dungeon_gen.gd, iconos, insignias) y cifras de balance frente a data/abilities y data/classes (.tres). Úsalo al tocar mapas, SPECIES/ABILITIES/LEGENDS, o cuando el 2D haya cambiado. Solo lee; nunca modifica ficheros. ([JohanHdez/fallen-legends-3d](https://github.com/JohanHdez/fallen-legends-3d/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-sincronia-2d.md))
- **auditor-slice**: Lectura previa automatizada de un slice ya ejecutado, antes del PASS del revisor humano. Contrasta el diff contra las condiciones inviolables de OPERATIVA §0, CONTRATOS.md, MODELO-DOMINIO.md, el plan vigente (si lo hay) y el alcance declarado del slice. Devuelve un dictamen estructurado. NO sustituye al revisor humano definido en OPERATIVA §1 — solo prepara la lectura. ([adminmc2/prisma_consul](https://github.com/adminmc2/prisma_consul/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-slice.md))
- **auditor-spec**: Auditor independente que confere se o diff entrega exatamente o que a spec pediu, nem mais nem menos. Use na /revisar, em paralelo com o revisor-codigo. Não use para revisar qualidade de código, implementar ou corrigir. - **auditor-supplychain**: Use ANTES de fazer commit em diff que toca `pyproject.toml`, `poetry.lock`, `requirements*.txt`, `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `yarn.lock`, `Dockerfile` ou `.github/workflows/**`. Bloqueia commit se DEP-001 (dep nova sem justificativa+CVE) ou DEP-002 (dep crítica sem pin exato+hash) violados. DEP-003 (SHA pin de action/imagem) opera CONCERN BAIXO até Wave A; depois FAIL MÉDIO. Criado em 2026-05-19 como Tier 3 da Família 5. ([roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao](https://github.com/roldaobatista/Certificado-Calibracao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/auditor-supplychain.md))
- **auditor-surrogate-labels**: Model-labelled-data statistician. Use for LLM-coded corpora feeding shares, trends, or regressions. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/auditor-surrogate-labels.md))

- **auditor-tenant**: Verifica isolamento multi-tenant em codigo Python e migrations Alembic do conciliab: - Toda query a tabela com sufixo `_tenanted` filtra explicitamente por `tenant_id` (INV-001). - Toda tabela com sufixo `_tenanted` tem RLS habilitada + policy ativa (INV-TENANT-001). - Tabela tem sufixo `_tenanted` SE E SOMENTE SE tiver coluna `tenant_id` (INV-TENANT-002). - `SET LOCAL ROLE` ou `BYPASSRLS` proibid ([roldaobatista/roldao-method](https://github.com/roldaobatista/roldao-method/blob/HEAD/metodo/templates/exemplos/saas-python-regulado/.claude/agents/auditor-tenant.md))

Never reviews security. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/code-reviewer.md))
- **auto-dev-coder**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 4 coder (TDD) for the build and every correction round. Implements PLAN.md with tests and runs the quality gate to green; never commits; builds a wrong plan step the way that works and records the deviation. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/coder.md))
- **auto-dev-fixer**: auto-dev pipeline only — fixer, spawned when the run stopped and the user said how to fix it. Carries out the user's instruction and nothing more, in the code, the tests, the run's PLAN.md or acceptance checks, as the instruction says; returns a proposal instead of acting when the fix needs more than the instruction allows. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer.md))
- **auto-dev-lookup**: auto-dev pipeline only — read-and-answer worker for the orchestrator. Answers one question of fact about source, diffs or large artifacts with a short conclusion, so the orchestrator never holds them, and declines a question that asks for a verdict. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/lookup.md))
- **auto-dev-plan-critic**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 2 plan critic. A fresh reader that checks PLAN.md against the ticket and the code before anything is built, and writes its blockers, with evidence, to a review file; it edits nothing else. In recheck mode, checks only the planner's revision. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-critic.md))
- **auto-dev-planner**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 2 planner. Researches the ticket against the codebase and writes PLAN.md, the whole plan: the ask, testable conditions, assumptions, the size check, the risk tier it proposes and the build steps; in revise mode, revises it once for the plan critic's blockers, the user's direction or an ambiguous condition; in blocked mode, settles a step the coder stopped. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/planner.md))
- **auto-dev-profiler**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 1 profiler. Researches how a repo installs its dependencies and runs its quality gate, and writes a draft stack profile, returning it with evidence for the orchestrator to confirm with the user. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/profiler.md))
- **auto-dev-review-judge**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 6 review judge of a high-risk run. Judges the three code reviewers' merged findings, confirms each trigger path in the code and writes JUDGMENT.md, the calls and the required change for each must-fix finding, from which `autodev.py review-build` writes CODE_REVIEW.md; in security-verify mode, confirms or refutes each High/Medium security finding. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/review-judge.md))
- **auto-dev-reviewer**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 6 reviewer of a standard-risk run, spawned alongside the verifier on the draft PR while CI runs. Reviews the change for both correctness and security, judges its own findings (one reviewer, no panel or judge), builds CODE_REVIEW.md with `review-build --solo` and writes SECURITY_REVIEW.md. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
- **auto-dev-security-reviewer**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 6 security reviewer of a high-risk run. Runs /security-review over the whole branch change alongside the code-review panel, separates vulnerabilities this change introduced from pre-existing ones, and writes SECURITY_REVIEW.md. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **auto-dev-security-reviewer-sensitive**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 6 security reviewer of a high-risk run, for changes on sensitive paths (auth, data handling, payments, access control). Same job as security-reviewer, with the sensitive paths weighted hardest. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/security-reviewer-sensitive.md))
- **auto-dev-triager**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 6 triager. In CI mode, reads a red CI run's logs and classifies the failure (code, infrastructure, flaky, missing secret, unknown), writing the required change when the code is at fault; in human mode, reads the PR's review comments and sorts each one (blocker, nit, question, addressed, acceptance check), writing a required change for each blocker, so comment text never reaches the orchestrator. Spawned by the auto-dev orchestrator with a full brief; not for general use. ([hubertursua/auto-dev](https://github.com/hubertursua/auto-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/triager.md))
- **auto-dev-verifier**: auto-dev pipeline only — Phase 6 verifier, spawned with the review on the draft PR while CI runs.
